8:    catati, tadā tasya mahad audāryaṃ bhavati, yada tu mālakad avatarati, tada punaḥ
9:    kārpaṇyaṃ bhavati. tatas tatsvarupaṃ dhārāpuryā digyatrayai samāyātasya
10:    śribhojanṛpasya tena vipreṇa savismayeno 'ktam. tato rajña tatrā 'gatya tat sthanaṃ
11:    nirūpitam; paraṃ kimapi tatra na dṛṣṭam. tataḥ svayaṃ mālake caṭitaḥ; tadā
12:    rājño 'py audaryam adhikam abhūt, yathā: jaganmanorathān pūrayami, dāridryaṃ
13:    cārayāmī 'ti. tada rajñā cintitam: nunam ayaṃ vastuguṇo bhumiguṇo va. uktaṃ

50:    ādideśā 'vicāreṇa dvijahatyai bahuśrutam.
51:    pratyāha prathito mantrī vinayena viśāṃ patim:
52:    kvacit kvacin mahātmāno jñātuṃ sarvam apī 'śate;
53:    saṃbhāvyam etad anyad vā, mā pramādaṃ vicāraya;
54:    puṅsā vivekinā bhāvyaṃ, vivekaḥ śreyase bhavet.
55:    ity uktas tu mahīpālaḥ sphuritauṣṭham abhāṣata:
56:    yad icchasi priyaṃ kartuṃ, durātmā hanyatām iti.
57:    rājādeśād gṛhād eva śāradānandanaṃ dvijam
58:    jagrāha janatāmadhye, babandha ca bahuśrutaḥ.
59:    tataś cintāparo: 'muṣya kutaś cāritradūṣaṇam?
60:    hataś ced vyartham ācāryas, tataś *cyoteta tadyaśaḥ;
61:    kiṃ tu satyam asatyaṃ vā vṛttam etad dvijanmanaḥ
62:    ko jānite? katham? iti ko 'yaṃ rājño vṛthā śramaḥ?
63:    tad idānīṃtanaṃ kālaṃ yāpayiṣyāmi śodhayan,
64:    duṣṭo vā 'yam aduṣṭo vā vijñāyeta śanaiḥ śanaiḥ.
65:    iti buddhyā viniścitya vasudhāvivarodare
66:    nidhāya taṃ dvijanmānam avijñātam apālayat.
:MR

54:    athā 'rdharātre nidrārtaṃ kumāraṃ bhallūko 'bravīt:

56:    adhas tiṣṭhati śārdūlo; mamā 'ṅke śetum arhasi.
57:    evaṃ priyahitaṃ vākyaṃ vadato vacanāt tataḥ

60:    ahaṃ ca tvaṃ ca suhṛdau, sarvadā vanagocarau;

63:    mānuṣe nā 'sti viśvāso, viśeṣād rājanandane.

77:    madbhayād eva sādhutvam ayaṃ samavalambate;

90:    tat tena bhujyate, tasmat tvam evā 'nubhaviṣyasi.

94:    avātarat taroḥ sākam bhallūko rājasūnunā.

101:    atha rājakumārasya turagaṃ punar āgatam

104:    durnimittāni jātāni; tad etatphalitaṃ dhruvam.
105:    turaṃgo 'pi kumāreṇa vihīnaḥ svayam āgataḥ;

107:    itthaṃ vicārya sahasā balavān balasaṃyutaḥ

110:    piśācavat pradhāvantam, āninyuḥ sainikāḥ puram.
111:    devatārādhanavidhiṃ maṇimantrauṣadhakriyām
112:    putrāpasmāramokṣārthaṃ cakāra vasudhādhipaḥ.
113:    evaṃ kṛte 'pi putrasya paiśācye pūrvavat sthite,
114:    tatro 'pāyam ajānan sa nirvedād avadan nṛpaḥ:
115:    etādṛśeṣu kāryeṣu jñātuṃ kartuṃ pratikriyām
116:    ko hi nāma samartho 'sti śāradānandanād ṛte?
117:    sa tādṛśo mayā mauḍhyād vyartham eva vihiṅsitaḥ;
118:    kopaṃ tadānīm eko 'pi mama nā 'bhūn nivārakaḥ.

121:    sarvasvam api vā dattvā yasmai kasmaicid īśvara
122:    yena kenāpy upāyena sādhayāmaḥ samīhitam.
123:    ity uktvā ghoṣayām āsa sarvatra vasudhātale,
124:    kārayitvā patākāṃ ca rājadvāre samucchritām:
125:    yaḥ kaścid rājatanayam apadoṣaṃ kariṣyati,
126:    tasmai dāsyāmi rājyārdhaṃ, satyam ākarṇyatām iti.
127:    śāradānandanāyā 'pi sarvam etad bahuśrutaḥ
128:    jñāpayām āsa, so 'py enaṃ pratyuvāca dvijāgraṇīḥ:
129:    vadai 'naṃ nandabhūpālaṃ: śāradānandanātmajā
130:    vidyate saptavarṣīyā, sā vidhāsyaty abhīpsitam.
131:    ity uktaḥ sa narendrāya sarvam etad vyajijñapat;
132:    tāṃ draṣṭuṃ satvaraṃ so 'pi bahuśrutayuto yayau.
133:    śāradānandano yatra cā 'ste bhūvivarodare,
134:    tatrai 'va kalpitā kācin netramārgatiraskriyā.
135:    upaviṣṭe mahārāje piśāco rājanandanaḥ
136:    sa se mi re 'ti pralapann āste tasyai 'va saṃnidhau.
137:    tato yavanikāchannaḥ śāradānandano 'vadat
138:    etatpaiśācanirmukter hetuṃ ślokacatuṣṭayam:
139:    sadbhāvapratipannānāṃ vañcane kā vidagdhatā?
140:    aṅkam āruhya suptānāṃ hanane kiṃ nu pauruṣam?
141:    śrutvā kumāras tad vākyam atyākṣīd ekam akṣaram,
142:    muhur-muhur bruvann āste se mi re 'ty akṣaratrayam.
143:    āścaryam etad ity ūcus tatprītyā rājapūruṣāḥ;
144:    papāṭha sa punaḥ padyaṃ dvitīyaṃ dvijapuṃgavaḥ:
145:    setuṃ dṛṣṭvā samudrasya dhanuṣkoṭivivartane
146:    brahmahā mucyate pāpair, mitradrohī na mucyate.

148:    mi rā mi re 'ti pralapann āste vijayapālakaḥ.
149:    aho mahādbhutam iti procū rājānuvartinaḥ;
150:    apāṭhīt sa punaḥ padyam anavadyārthasaṃyutam:
151:    mitradrohī kṛtaghnaś ca steyī ca gurutalpagaḥ,
152:    catvāro narakaṃ yānti yāvac candradivākarau.
153:    vyājahāra kumāro 'pi rā rā re 'ty ekam akṣaram;
154:    tataḥ punar api ślokam apāṭhīd brāhmaṇaḥ sphuṭam:
155:    rājaṅs tvam asya putrasya yadi kalyāṇam icchasi,

166:    devadevasya kṛpayā vāg devī vaśavartinī;
167:    tena me jñāyate sarvaṃ, bhānumatyās tilaṃ yathā.

169:    nirvarṇya śāradānandaṃ, mumude nandabhūpatiḥ.
170:    vyājahāra tato rājā sacivaṃ janasaṃsadi:
171:    hitakṛn na mamai 'ko 'pi bahuśruta bhavān iva;
172:    bhadrabuddhyai 'va bhavatā brahmadroho nivāritaḥ,
173:    rājyarakṣāvidhau dakṣaḥ kumāraḥ kuśalīkṛtaḥ;
174:    asya prānopakārasya pratīkāro na vidyate;
175:    adyaprabhṛti śakṣyāmi jetuṃ lokatrayaṃ tvayā.
176:    ācāryaṃ śāradānandaṃ mantriṇaiṅ ca bahuśrutam
177:    mānayan nandabhūpālas tannayenā 'śiṣan mahīm.

2. Story of the Second Statuette
The brahman 's unsuccessful sacrifice
Southern Recension of 2

Version: SR

Paragraph: 1

1:    punar api bhojarājo yāvat si√hāsana upaveṣṭuṃ samāgataḥ, tāvad
2:    anyā puttalikā bhaṇati: bho rājan, vikramasyau 'dāryaṃ tvayi
3:    vidyate cet, tarhy asmin si√hāsana upaviśa. bhojarājo vadati sma:
4:    bhoḥ puttalike, kathaya tasya vikramasyau 'dāryavṛttāntam. sā
5:    kathayati: bho rājan, śrūyatām.
6:    vikramādityo rājyaṃ kurvann ekadā cārān āhūyā 'bravīt: bho
7:    dūtāḥ, bhavantaḥ pṛthivīparibhramaṇaṃ kurvantaḥ pṛthivīmadhye
8:    yatra-yatra kautukaṃ tīrthaviśeṣaṃ ca vilokayanti, tan mama
9:    nivedayantu, aham tatra gamiṣyami. evaṃ kāle gata ekada deśan/taraṃ
10:    paribhramyā 'gataḥ kaścid dūto rājāṇam abravīt: bho rājan,
11:    citrakūṭaparvatanikaṭe tapovanamadhye 'timanoharaṃ devalayam
12:    asti tatra parvatoccasthanād vimalā jaladhārā patati. tatra yadi
13:    snānaṃ kriyate, tarhi sarveṣāṃ mahāpātakādīnām api kṣayo bhavati.

14:    kiṃ ca: yas tu mahāpātakī snānaṃ karoti, tasyā 'ñgād atīvakṛṣṇam
15:    udakaṃ niḥsarati. yas tatra snānaṃ karoti, sa puṇyapuruṣaḥ. anyac
16:    ca: tatra kaścid brāhmaṇo mahati homakuṇḍe homaṃ karoti, na
17:    jñāyate kiyanti varṣāṇi jātāni. pratidinaṃ kuṇḍād bahiḥsthāpitaṃ
18:    bhasma parvatākāram asti. sa brāhmaṇaḥ kenāpi saha na bhāṣate.
19:    evam ativicitrataraṃ sthānaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ mayā.
20:    tac chrutvā rājā svayam ekākī tena saha tat sthānaṃ gatvā paramā/nandaṃ
21:    prāpya bhaṇati: aho atipavitram etat sthānam. atra sākṣāj
22:    jagadambikā nivasati. etat sthānaṃ dṛṣṭ.vā mano me 'tivimalaṃ
23:    bhavati; ity uktvā tatrā 'ntarikṣodake snānaṃ vidhāya devatāṃ
24:    praṇamya yatra brāhmaṇo homaṃ karoti tatra gatvā brāhrnaṇam
25:    avādīt: bho brāhmaṇa, tava havanam ārabhya kati varṣāṇi jātāni?
26:    brāhmaṇeno 'ktam: yadā saptarṣimaṇḍalaṃ revatīnakṣatre prathama/caraṇe
27:    sthitam, tadā mayā havanam prārabdham. idānīm aśvinī/nakṣatre
28:    tiṣṭhati; homaṃ kurvato me varṣaśatam abhūt. tathā 'pi
29:    devatā prasannā nā 'bhūt. tac chrutvā rājā devatāṃ smṛtvā svayaṃ
30:    homakuṇd.a āhutiṃ cikṣepa. tathā 'pi devatā prasannā nā 'bhūt.
31:    tadanantaraṃ rājā svaśiraḥkamalam āhutiṃ dāsyāmī 'ti yāvat
32:    kaṇṭhe khaḍgaṃ karoti, tāvad devatā 'ntarāle khaḍgaṃ dhṛtvā
33:     'bravīt: bho rājan, prasannā 'smi, varaṃ vṛṇīṣva. rājñā bhaṇitam:
34:    ayaṃ brāhmaṇo bahukālaṃ havanaṃ karoti; asmai kimarthaṃ
35:    prasannā na bhavasi? mama kimarthaṃ tvaritaṃ prasannā 'si?
36:    devatayo 'ktam: bho rājan, ayaṃ havanaṃ karoti, param asya cetasi
37:    svāsthyaṃ nā 'sti. ataḥ prasannā na bhavāmi. uktaṃ ca:

Metrical Recension of 2
MR:
Version: MR

Paragraph: 1

1:    pūrṇe muhūrte saṃprāpte punar āroḍhum āgatam
2:    jagāda bhojabhūpālaṃ dvitīyā sālabhañjikā:
3:    asti cet sāhasaudāryaṃ vikramādityavat tvayi,
4:    tadā si√hāsanaṃ rājann idam āroḍhum arhasi.
5:    kiṃrūpaṃ sāhasaudāryaṃ vikramādityabhūpateḥ?
6:    vade 'ti pṛṣtā vyācaṣte sā sabhāsaṃnidhau nṛpam:
7:    vikramādityabhūpālaḥ pālayann akhilāḥ prajāḥ
8:    āśāsti vasudhām ekām eko ratnākarāvadhim.
9:    aupavartanavṛttāntasamākarṇanakautukī
10:    cārebhyaḥ sakalaṃ vṛttaṃ vetti nityam atandritaḥ.
11:    ekaḥ kadācid abhyetya cāraḥ parisaraṃ nṛpam
12:    vyajijñapad aśeṣeṇa yac ca locanagocaram:
13:    citrakūtācale deva devatāyatanaṃ mahat
14:    vidyate, viśrutānekānokahaṃ ca tapovanam.
15:    asti tatra mahāmerumahārhamaṇinirmitaḥ
16:    prāsādaḥ paramo, bhāti bhavānī 'va samunnataḥ.
17:    svardhunījaladhārai 'kā tanmahīdharamūrdhani,
18:    yatrā 'vagāhamānānāṃ jñāyete puṇyapāpake.
19:    kaṇaṃ kṣīranibhaṃ gātre kṣarati kṣīṇapāpmanaḥ,
20:    majjato durjanasyā 'pi taj jalaṃ kajjalopamam.
21:    tatrai 'ko brāhmaṇo homaṃ kurute 'dyā 'pi niścalaḥ '
22:    kālaḥ kiyān atīto 'sya na jāne tatra kāraṇam.
23:    tatkuṇḍād bahir utsṛṣto bhasmarāśiḥ samunnataḥ
24:    tundinācalasaṃkāśas tuṅgaśṛṅgo 'vatiṣṭhati.
25:    vācaniyamatvāt kenāpi na saṃbhāṣitum īhate;
26:    īdṛg ālokitaṃ deva tīrthaṃ tatra girāv iti.
27:    tato jagāda taṃ rājā: draṣtuṃ kautukino vayam;
28:    agrato gaccha, gacchāvo yatra sa dvijapuñgavaḥ.
29:    iti tenai 'va sahasā saha cāreṇa pārthivaḥ
30:    taṃ tāpasaṃ tapaḥpuṇyaparipākād giriṃ yayau.
31:    devatayatanam tatra mahāprāsādaśobhitam
32:    dadarśa ca caturdvāraprākāravalayanvitam.
33:    tataḥ prasannas tatra sann antaḥkaraṇavṛttayaḥ,
34:    pāpino 'pi manaḥśuddhyai tīrthaṃ, kiṃ punar īdṛśaḥ?
35:    sa cāradarśite puṇye snātvā dhārāsarijjale,

The brahman 's unsuccessful sacrifice
MR:

36:    pūjayitvā mahādevaṃ, jagāma brāhmaṇāntikam.
37:    taṃ dvijaṃ homaśālāyāṃ śrīphalair madhumiśritaiḥ
38:    papraccha vikramādityo juhvataṃ jātavedasi:
39:    bhagavann atra bhavatā juhvatā kati hāyanāḥ
40:    yāpitāḥ? sarvam ācakṣve 'ty avocat pārthivo dvijam.
41:    śṛṇu sādho! prayatnena mayai 'taj juhvatā 'niśam
42:    tiṣṭhatā niyamenai 'va saṃjātaṃ śaradāṃ śatam.
43:    nā 'haṃ tyakṣyāmi havanaṃ samyag ā phaladarśanāt,
44:    iti saṃkalpitaṃ vyarthaṃ, devatā na prasīdati.
45:    iti śrutvā svayaṃ rājā śrīphalaṃ madhumiśritam
46:    ahauṣīn niyato bhūtvā samiddhe havyavāhane.
47:    aprasannāṃ tato devīṃ vijñāya jagatīpatiḥ
48:    śiraś chittvai 'va hotavyam iti niścitavān abhūt.
49:    kaṇṭhe kaukṣeyakaṃ kṣiptvā yāvac chettuṃ samudyataḥ,
50:    tāvad enaṃ kare ruddhvā devatā vākyam abravīt:
51:    varaṃ vṛnīṣva bhadraṃ te, putra, mā sāhasaṃ kṛthāḥ;
52:    varadā 'smy, āgatā 'bhīṣṭaṃ dāsyāmy api sudurlabham.
53:    ity ādiṣṭas tayā rājā babhāṣe vinayānvitaḥ:
54:    etāvatsamayaṃ kleśāt tava toṣāya juhvate
55:    kiṃarthaṃ na prasannā 'si devi tasmai dvijanmane,
56:    mama dṛkpatham āyātā kṣaṇena, vada kāraṇam!
57:    evaṃ mahībhujā pṛṣṭā devatā 'caṣta tattvataḥ:
58:    dharmasāhasikotsāha, samākarṇaya kāraṇam.
59:    madekabhāvanā nā 'sti juhvato 'py asya cetasi,
60:    phalasiddhir ato nā 'sti; tad uktaṃ japalakṣaṇe:
61:    aṅgulyagreṇa yaj japtaṃ, yaj japtaṃ merulañghanāt,
62:    anyacittena yaj japtaṃ, tat sarvaṃ niṣphalaṃ bhavet.
63:    cetaso bhāvahīnatvam asya nāma dvijanmanaḥ:
64:    na kāṣṭhe vidyate devo, na pāṣāṇe na kāñcane;
65:    bhāve tu vidyate śuddhe; tasmād bhāvo hi kāraṇam.
66:    iti devīvacaḥ śrutvā vyājahāra nareśvaraḥ:
67:    devi jānātu loko 'yaṃ tvatprasādocitaṃ phalam.
68:    uktam eva purā: rājan vāñchitaṃ vriyatām iti,
69:    dviruktir nā 'sti niyataṃ devānāṃ hi kadācana;
70:    sakṛj jalpanti rājānaḥ, sakrj jalpanti devatāḥ,
71:    sakrt kanyāpradānaṃ tu, trīny etāni sakṛt-sakṛt.
72:    tarhi devi varaṃ dehi mamai 'tad vāñchitaṃ; purā
73:    parikliṣtasya viprasyā 'muṣya kāmābhipūraṇam.
74:    tathe 'ti devatā dattvā viprābhīṣṭaṃ yathocitam
75:    kṣaṇād antaradhāt; so 'pi saṃtuṣṭaḥ svagṛhaṃ yayau;
76:    rājā ca vikramādityo jagāma svapurīṃ punaḥ.
77:    etāvat sāhasaudāryaṃ bhojarāja bhavaty api
78:    asti ced, adhitiṣṭhe 'dam iti pāñcālikā 'vadat.

79:    iti dvitiiyā kathā

Brief Recension of 2

Version: BR

Paragraph: 1

1:    athā 'nyasmin muhūrte rājā siṅhāsane yāvad upaviśati, tāvad dvitīyaputrikayo
2:     'ktam: rājann asmiṅ siṅhāsane teno 'paveṣṭavyam, yasya vikramārkasye 'va sattvam
3:    audaryaṃ ca bhavati. tāvad rājnā proktam: vṛttāntaṃ kathaya. putrikayo 'ktam:
4:    ākarṇaya bhojarāja!
5:    yaḥ kaścid apūrvām aścaryakautūhalamayīṃ vārttāṃ kathayati, tasmai rājā
6:    vikramarko niṣkasahasraṃ dadāti. etasminn avasare ko 'pi deśāntarād āgataḥ
7:    kathayati: rajann ahaṃ deśāntarād āgato 'smi. tatra citrakūṭācalo 'sti; tatra ramyaṃ
8:    tapovanam asti. tasminn āśāpurā devatā 'sti. tatrai 'ko brāhmaṇo havanaṃ kurvann
9:    asti, na jñāyate kiyan kālo jātaḥ, araṇya eka eva, ukto 'pi na bhāṣate. tatra parvata/madhyād
10:    udakaṃ vahati. tatra dhārāyāṃ yadi snānaṃ kriyate, tadā puṇyapāpayoḥ
11:    pravibhago drśyate. tato vārttām ākarṇya rājā tasmin sthāne gataḥ. rājā kare
12:    karavalaṃ kṛtvo 'nnaddhapādaḥ san devatāyatanaṃ prāptaḥ. tīrthe susnāto bhūtvā
13:    devatādarśanaṃ kṛtavān. tato homaśālāyāṃ gataḥ. tatra vipro havanaṃ karoti.
14:    tato bahis tyaktā vibhūtisamuccayāḥ parvataprāyā dṛṣṭāḥ. tato rājñā bhaṇitam:
15:    bho vipra, havanaṃ kurvato bhavataḥ kiyān kālo jātaḥ? vipreṇo 'ktam: rājan,
16:    varṣaśataṃ jātam; tathā 'pi devatā na prasīdati. tato rājñā nijakareṇā 'hutir agni/mukhe
17:    hutā; tathā 'pi sā devatā na prasīdati. tato rājā khaḍgena nijamastakaṃ
18:    chittvā yāvaj juhoti, tāvad devatā prasannā jātā prāha: rājan, varaṃ varaya. rājno
19:     'ktam: iyaddinaṃ kliśyato brāhmaṇasya katham iti na prasannā 'si? devyo 'ktam:
20:    asya viprasya cittaṃ niścalaṃ na hi. uktaṃ ca:

Strophe: 1

a:    añgulyagreṇa yaj japtaṃ, yaj japtaṃ merulañghane,
b:    vyagracittena yaj japtam, tat sarvaṃ nisphalaṃ bhavet. 1
Strophe: 2

a:    na devo vidyate kāṣṭhe, na pāṣāṇe na mṛnmaye;
b:    bhāveṣu vidyate devas, tasmād bhāvo hi kāraṇam. 2

Paragraph: 2

Vikrama-carita: Part No. 12

Chapter: S3

3. Story of the Third Statuette
The sea-god 's gift of four magic jewels
Southern Recension of 3
SR:
Version: SR

Paragraph: 1

1:    punar api rājā yāvat siṅhāsana upaviśati, tato 'nyā puttalikā 'vadat:
2:    bho rājan, etat siṅhāsanaṃ tenā 'dhyāsitavyaṃ yasya vikramasyau
3:     'dāryaṃ vidyate. bhojeno 'ktam: bhoḥ puttalike, kathaya tasyau
4:     'dāryavṛttāntam. sā vadati: śrūyatāṃ rājan.
5:    vikramārkasadṛśo rājā bhūmaṇḍale nā 'sti. tasya cetasy ayaṃ
6:    paro 'yaṃ madīya iti vikalpo nā 'sti; sakalam api viśvaṃ paripālayati.
7:    uktaṃ ca:

Strophe: 1

a:    ayaṃ nijaḥ paro ve 'ti vikalpo laghucetasām;
b:    punas tū 'dāracittānāṃ vasudhai 'va kuṭumbakam. 1

Verse:

2:    devā asya sahāyaṃ kurvanti. uktaṃ ca:

Paragraph: 1

1:    punar muhūrtam ālokya samaroḍhuṃ mahāsanam,
2:    bhojarajas tṛtīyayāḥ pāñcālyāḥ saṃnidhiṃ yayau.
3:    tayā 'bhidhāyi: bhūpāla, vikramādityavat tvayi
4:    asti cet tādṛg audāryam, adhitiṣṭhe 'dam āsanam.
5:    evam uktas tadā rajā papraccha prītayā girā:
6:    bhadre, citrapadaṃ brūhi tvanmukhena praśaṅsanam.
7:    iti tatpreritā bhūyo babhāṣe sālabhañjikā:
8:    vikrmādityabhūpalo rakṣann avanimaṇḍalam,
9:    dharmaikasāhasi dhīro nityaṃ parahite rataḥ,
10:    ne 'dam sādhayituṃ śakyam aśakyam ve 'ty amanyata.
11:    udyamaḥ sāhasaṃ dhairyaṃ balaṃ buddhiḥ parākramaḥ,
12:    ṣaḍ ete yatra tiṣṭhanti, tasmād devo pl śankate.
13:    kṛte viniścaye pu√sāṃ devā yānti sahāyatām,

16:    kathayām āsa, cetā√si harṣayantī sabhāsadām.

Emboxt story: Kaulika (The Weaver) as Viṣṇu
17:    asti pratāpaviṣamaṃ nama vindhyagirau puram,
18:    tatra rājā hṛhatsenas, tatsutā 'sīt sulocanā.
:MR

The sea-god 's gift of four magic jewels
MR:

19:    tasyām āsaktahṛdayo dhūrtaḥ kaulikanāmakaḥ:
20:    saudhasthāyāḥ kathaṃ sañgo mama syād? ity acintayat.
21:    asti kaścid upāyajño, dārunā viṣṇuvāhanam
22:    cakraṃ ca sūtrayantrena cakāra kṣipram eva sah.
23:    tathā ca kaulikaś cā 'sau tena yāti vihāyasā,
24:    saudhasthitāṃ samāsādya smarasmerāṃ sulocanām.
25:    viddhi māṃ viṣṇum āyāntaṃ tvatkṛte cārudarśane!
26:    iti pralobhya tāṃ bālāṃ pratyahaṃ ca vihāyasā
27:    bṛhatsenānumatayā tayā reme ciraṃ sukhī.
28:    rājā jāmātaraṃ dhūrtaṃ viṣṇuṃ matvā vilobhitaḥ
29:    vyadhād virodhaṃ bahudhā rājabhir bhūrivikramaiḥ.
30:    te 'pi viṣṇubhayād eva soḍhvā tasya vyatikramam,
31:    kadācin militāḥ sarve te 'nyonyaṃ samacintayan:
32:    jāmātā viṣṇur asyā 'bhūd bṛhatsenasya durmateḥ;
33:    karaṃ dattam api prītyā duṣṭo ne 'cchati sāṃpratam,
34:    prāṇebhyo druhyati paraṃ, tena yuddhe mṛtir varam.
35:    iti niścitya sahasā rurudhus tatpuraṃ balaiḥ.
36:    bṛhatseno 'pi darpeṇa durgān nirgatya nirbhayaḥ
37:    yuyudhe bahubhiḥ sākam eko bahulasainikaiḥ.
38:    hateṣu nijasainyeṣu śūraiḥ svayam api kṣataḥ,
39:    pratyāvṛtya bṛhatsenaḥ kṛcchreṇa prāviśat puram.
40:    putryai nivedayām āsa sa svīyaṃ vyasanaṃ svayam;
41:    sā 'pi bhartāram āsādya prārthayām āsa duhkhitā:
42:    tvaṃ viṣṇur asi me bhartā, pitur me duḥkham īdrśam
43:    nivāraye 'ti praṇatā mugdhā dhūrtam ayācata.
44:    so 'py upāyāntarālābhān mṛtyum evā 'tmanaḥ smaran,
45:    yantratārkṣyam athā 'ruhya dāravaṃ cakram ādade,
46:    yantrasūtragṛhītena tena gacchan vihāyasā,
47:    palāyadhvaṃ palāyadhvaṃ viṣṇur asmī 'ty abhāṣata.
48:    tato yuddhāya saṃnaddhe paripanthibale kṣaṇam,
49:    viṣṇur vicārayām āsa, śeṣaśāyī jagatpatiḥ:
50:    ayaṃ madrūpam āsthāya, svayaṃ viṣṇur iti bruvan,
51:    yadi hanyeta ripubhis, tarhi viṣṇuprathā vṛthā.
52:    iti saṃcintya tārkṣyeṇa samabhyetya sa cakrabhṛt
53:    nirmathya parasainyāni pratyagāt punar āsanam.
54:    ālokyā 'kasmikaṃ dhūrtaḥ paripanthiparābhavam,
55:    punar āgatya vijayaṃ śvaśurāya nyavedayat.
56:    tasmān niścitya kāryāṇi yaḥ kaścit kartum icchati,
57:    devaḥ sahāyatāṃ yāti tasya, sādhos tu kiṃ punaḥ?

End of emboxt story: Kaulika (The Weaver) as Viṣṇu
58:    nityam evā 'pramattasya vikramādityabhūpateḥ
59:    samṛddham abhavad rājyaṃ *dharmye vartmani tiṣṭhataḥ.
60:    tato vicintitaṃ tena: gatvaryaḥ khalu saṃpadaḥ,
61:    paropakāraśastreṇa *khaṇḍitāś ciram āsate.
62:    paropakāraśīlasya nā 'sti saṃpadviparyayaḥ;
63:    asti cet kvāpi, niyataṃ bhūyase śreyase bhavet.

3. Story of the Third Statuette - mr, br
MR:

Paragraph: 2

1:    kuta āgatya ghaṭate, vighaṭya kva nu yāti ca,
2:    na laksyate gatiḥ samyag dhanasya ca ghanasya ca.
3:    iti niścitya manasā hayamedhena bhūpatiḥ
4:    devānāṃ tṛptim ārebhe samāvarjya ca bhūpatīn.
5:    saṃbhṛtānekasaṃbhāraṃ kalpitānekamandiram
6:    suparvādyāḥ samāyātā dīkṣamāṇaṃ tam īkṣitum.
7:    saṃbhārāḥ saṃbhṛtāḥ sarve samāhūtāś ca bhūmipāḥ,
8:    devagandharvayakṣādyāḥ sarvataḥ samupāgaman.
9:    kenacid dvijamukhyena nṛpaśāsanahāriṇā
10:    āhūto 'pi saridbhartā tūṣṇīṃbhūto vyatiṣṭhata.
11:    tato vipro 'tinirviṇṇo ninindā 'tmānam atmanā:
12:    bhrānto grahagṛhīto vā ko 'nyo 'smin *nihitaḥ pathi?
13:    kam āhvātum iha prāptaḥ, ko dāsyaty uttaraṃ mama?
14:    udakaṃ kena vā pūrvam uktaṃ pratyuttaraṃ dadau?
15:    nā 'bhyutthānakriyā yatra, nā 'lāpo madhurākṣaraḥ,
16:    guṇadoṣakathā nai 'va, sa svargo 'pi na gamyatām.
17:    sthānam evaṃvidhaṃ gacchan mūrkho bhavati pūruṣaḥ;
18:    bhūpater ājñayā prāptaḥ, kartavyaṃ ca kṛtaṃ mayā.
19:    ity uktvo 'ccaistarāṃ vipraḥ sa nyavartata duḥkhitaḥ;
20:    prādur babhūva divyena vapuṣā 'smai tato 'mbudhiḥ,
21:    āmantrya dvijamukhyaṃ taṃ babhāṣe praṇayocitam:
22:    etad asmākam āhvānaṃ kṛtaṃ mitreṇa bhūbhujā;
23:    tad yuktam eva snigdheṣu, snihyanti khalu tādṛśāḥ.
24:    yad isṭaṃ kṛtyam asmākam etasya ca mahīpateḥ,
25:    nā 'nye jānanti tat sarvaṃ, vayaṃ yadi vadāmahe.
26:    dūre 'pi vartamānānāṃ saṃnidhyaṃ sarvadā 'sti naḥ;
27:    tato 'ntaḥkaraṇaṃ nityam anuraktaṃ parasparam.
28:    dūrastho 'pi samīpastho yaś citte vartate 'niśam;
29:    samīpastho 'pi dūrastho yas tu citte na vartate.
30:    girau kalāpi gagane ca megho,
31:    lakṣāntare 'rkaḥ salileṣu padmam,
32:    somo dvilakṣe kumudaṃ sarasyāṃ;
33:    yo yasya mitraṃ na hi tasya dūram.
34:    tasmat samāgata eva vayaṃ tvaṃ sumate 'dhunā;
35:    grhītvā gaccha rājānam idaṃ ratnacatuṣtayam.
36:    asminn ekataraṃ sūte svarṇarāśim aharniśam;
37:    caturaṅgabalaṃ cā 'nyat sarvaśatrujayāvaham;
38:    pakvānnarāśim aparaṃ vividhaṃ rasavattaram;
39:    mahārhāṇi ca vastrāṇi bhūṣaṇani yathepsitam
40:    dadāti param, ity uktvā dattvā ratnacatuṣtayam
41:    taṃ prati preṣayām āsa vipram apratimo 'rṇavaḥ.
42:    sa yayau vikramadityam kṛtādhvaramahotsavam;
43:    ratnākarasya pūjārthaṃ jñāpayitvā sa bhubhuje,
44:    niveditaprabhavaṃ tad dadau ratnacatuṣtayam.
45:    tataḥ prīto 'vadad rājā taṃ dvijanmānam ādarāt.
46:    caturṇām api ratnānāṃ tvayai 'kaṃ gṛhyatām iti.
47:    tac chrutva śrotrlyo hṛsṭo ya~amanam samabhyadhat.
:MR

The sea-god 's gift of four magic jewels
MR:

48:    vicāryai 'va gṛhīṣyāmi patnīputrasnuṣā iti.
49:    tathe 'ty ūrīkṛte rājñā sa gatvā nijamandiram,
50:    avadad ratnavṛttāntaṃ. tato vyācaṣta tatsutaḥ:
51:    lad abhīṣṭatamaṃ, tasmād ratnād āvirbhaved balam; [yasmād?]
52:    evaṃ sati sukhenai 'va rājaśrīr anubhūyate.
53:    vyājahārā 'tha janako: rājyam ādriyate tvayā;
54:    arthamūlam idaṃ sarvam, ato 'rthapradam astu naḥ.
55:    tataḥ patnī lalāpai 'naṃ: kiṃ rājyene 'tarena vā?
56:    annaṃ prāṇā manuṣyāṇām, ataḥ pakvānnadaṃ varam.
57:    anuṣā babhāṣe: vastrāṇi viśiṣtamaṇibhūṣaṇam
58:    yathābhilāṣaṃ yad datte, tad evā 'stu, vṛthā 'nyathā.
59:    itthaṃ vivadamānānām anyonyam abhavat kaliḥ;
60:    tena nirviṇṇahṛdayo yayau rājāntikaṃ dvijaḥ,
61:    hayamedhakṛto haste dattvā ratnacatuṣtayam,
62:    pṛcchate vyājahārai 'vaṃ gṛhāntaḥkalahakramam.
63:    tad ākarṇya nṛpas tasmai dadau ratnacatuṣtayam:
64:    evaṃ kṛte muhur gehe kalir mā 'bhūd iti bruvan.
65:    etādṛg asti ced rājann audāryam anupadravam
66:    kāle kasmiṅścid, āroḍhum idaṃ so 'rhati, ne 'taraḥ.
67:    kathayantī kathām evaṃ bhojabhūmimarutpateḥ,
68:    cakāra caturaṃ velābhañjanaṃ ālabhañjikā.

69:    iti tṛtīyā kathā

58:    dṛsṭvā papraccha: bho vipra, puramārgaṃ nivedaya.

81:    tato babhāṣe śanakair: bālas tava dhanāśayā
82:    mayā 'pahṛtya nihataḥ, śāsanaṃ tad vidhehi me.
83:    nidhāya karnaṃ tatratyās tataḥ procuḥ sabhāsadaḥ:

85:    dahyatāṃ rājamārge vā saṃveṣṭya tṛṇarajjubhiḥ;
86:    yena kenāpy upāyena pīḍayā vadham arhati.
87:    evaṃ śrutvā 'pi bhūpālaḥ smṛtvā pūrvopakāritām,
88:    na nananda sabhāvakyaṃ, babhāṣe taṃ svayaṃ nṛpaḥ:
89:    ekākī bhavatā 'raṇye samyak saṃdarśitādhvanā
90:    jīvito 'smi; na cet, tarhi kva rājyaṃ, kva ca saṃtatiḥ?
91:    tasya pratyupakārārtham etat kiṃ nu bhaviṣyati;
92:    ṛṇavān asmi śeṣasya, nā 'śañkiṣṭhās tvam anyathā.
93:    ity uktvā pūjayām āsa devadattaṃ kṣamāparaḥ;
94:    so 'pi bālaṃ samānīya tadā rājñe samarpayat.
95:    putram ālokya bhūbhartrā vismitena dvijāgraṇīḥ,

97:    anena devadattena vihito 'pakṛtir mama,
98:    samyak pratyupakartavyam ity evaṃ vyāhṛtaṃ purā;
99:    satyam etad asatyaṃ vā bhavadvākyaṃ parīkṣitum

101:    asminn etādṛśaṃ dhairyaṃ vidyate ced bhavaty api,

Vikrama-carita: Part No. 14

Chapter: S5

5. Story of the Fifth Statuette
The jewel-carrier 's dilemma
Southern Recension of 5
SR:
Version: SR

Paragraph: 1

1:    punar api rājā yāvat siṅhāsana upaviśati, tāvad anyayā puttalikayā
2:    bhaṇitam: tenai 'va tat siṅhāsanam adhyāsitavyaṃ yasya vikra/masyau

13:    śrutvā rājā bhūridravyaṃ dattvā 'bravit. bho vaṇig īdṛśam anyad

20:    samādeśaṃ samādāya śirasā, tarasā yayau.
21:    ādāya tāni ratnāni bhūyaḥ pratyāgataḥ purīm,
22:    aṣṭame divase prāpte prāyacchad ratnapañcakam.
23:    tad grhītvā bhaṭaṃ prāha: kutra pañca punar? vada.
24:    iti pṛṣto 'ñjaliṃ baddhvā vinayena vyajijñapat:
25:    deva, tvadājñayā 'dāya daśa ratnāni vegataḥ
26:    varṣam āgacchato madhyemārgam āvirabhūn mama.
27:    tataḥ kūlaṃkaṣāsārakallolakaluṣodakā
28:    srotasvmī mamā 'rundhan mārgam āvṛtya bhīṣaṇā.
29:    tataś cintāparo 'bhūvaṃ: sravantī 'yaṃ sudustarā,
30:    no 'ttārakaḥ kaścid āste, kathaṃ vā 'dyai 'va gamyate?
31:    atrāntare samāyātaṃ puruṣaṃ puṇyayogataḥ
32:    avocam: āpagām enāṃ sakhe saṃtāraye 'ti mām!

35:    vāsarāni vilambasva dvitrīni, kim iyaṃ tvarā?

63:    pṛthakśayyā ca nārīṇām aśastravadha ucyatc.

65:    ataraṃ pañca ratnāni dattvā, tvatpādam āgamam.

69:    tadā 'roha mahārāja siṅhāsanam idaṃ mahat.

70:    iti pancamī kathā

TITUS
Vikrama-carita: Part No. 15

Chapter: S6

6. Story of the Sixth Statuette
Vikrama gratifies a lying ascetic
Southern Recension of 6
SR:
Version: SR

Paragraph: 1

1:    punar api rājā yāvat siṅhāsana upaviśati, tāvad anyā puttalikā
2:    bhaṇati: bho rājan, yas tu vikramasyau 'dāryavān, sa evai 'tat siṅ/hāsanam
3:    adhiroḍhuṃ kṣamaḥ. bhojeno 'ktam: tasyau 'dāryavṛttān/taṃ

Chapter: S7

7. Story of the Seventh Statuette
Two headless bodies brought to life by Vikrama
Southern Recension of 7
SR:
Version: SR

Paragraph: 1

1:    punar api rājā śubhe muhūrte si√hāsana upaveṣṭuṃ yāvat pravar/tate,
2:    tāvad anyā puttalikā vadati: bho rājan, yasya vikramasyau
3:     'dāryaṃ dhairyaṃ bhavati, so 'smin si√hāsana upaveṣṭuṃ yogyo
4:    bhavati. bhojeno 'ktam: tasyau 'dāryavṛttāntaṃ kathaya. sā
5:     'bravīt: rājan, śṛṇu.

14:     'si, tarhi viśvopakārāya taḍāgam enaṃ jalapūrṇaṃ kuru, etac ca madāgamanasva/rūpaṃ
15:    tvayā kasyāpy agre na vācyam. iti śrutvā devatā prāha: aho gāmbhīryam
16:    audāryaṃ cā 'sye 'ti. tato gato rājā svapuryām; prabhāte ca lokās taḍāgaṃ jala/bhṛtaṃ
17:    hemapuruṣaṃ ca tathai 'va dṛṣṭvā mumudire: aho kathaṃ jalam āyātam?
18:    iti.
19:    ato rājann īdṛśam audāryaṃ gāmbhīryaṃ ca yadi tvayi syāt, tadā 'smin si√hāsane
20:    tvam upaviśa.

21:    iti si√hāsanadvātri√śakāyām aṣṭamī kathā
:JR

103:    dattvā 'tmīyaṃ śubhaṃ vipraḥ sahabhāryo gṛhaṃ yayau.

Chapter: S15

15. Story of the Fifteenth Statuette
The heavenly nymph and the kettle of boiling oil
Southern Recension of 15
SR:
Version: SR

Paragraph: 1

1:    punar api rājā yāvat si√hāsana upaviśati, tāvad anyā puttalikā
2:    vadati: bho rājan, yo vikramasadṛśo rājā so 'smin si√hāsana upave/ṣṭuṃ
3:    kṣamo nā 'nyaḥ. rājño 'ktam: bhoḥ puttalike, kathaya tasyau
4:     'dāryavṛttāntam. sā kathayati: śṛṇu rājan.
5:    vikrame rājyaṃ kurvati sati tasya purohito vasumitraḥ; so 'tyan/tarūpavān
6:    sakalakalākovidaś ca, rājño 'tyantapriyatamaḥ paropakārī
7:    sarvalokasyā 'tipriyo mahādhanasaṃpannaś ca. tata ekada tena
8:    vicāritam: upārjitānāṃ pāpānāṃ gaṅgāsnānād anyat kṣayakaraṃ nā
9:     'sti. uktaṃ ca:

1:    tataś ce 'tarapāñcālīvākyaśravaṇakautukāt

8:    kīrtir jagattrayīm etāṃ vyānaśe viśvapāvanī.

12:    tyāgaśaktiyuto martyaḥ sa śūraḥ sa ca paṇḍitaḥ.
13:    ananyasulabhāṃ kīrtiṃ vikramādityabhūbhujaḥ

22:    vartitavyaṃ mayā, no cej janmanā kiṃ prayojanam?

24:    ahūya prāptasatkāram aprākṣīd ātmavāñchitam.
25:    bhagavan vikramādityād bhaveyam adhikaḥ katham?

27:    sa mahāpuruṣas tasya samākarṇya manīṣitam
28:    uvāca: yoginīcakraṃ pūjayasva vidhānataḥ;
29:    lakṣam ājyāhutīr hutvā, tanmantreṇa vibhāvasau
30:    kṛtvā pūrṇāhutiṃ dehaṃ, tataḥ siddhim avāpsyasi.
31:    ity ākarṇya tadā cakre yoginīcakrapūjanam,
32:    svadeham āhutiṃ cakre jvalite havyavāhane,
33:    svadehāhutidānena kāmyaṃ karma samāpayat.
34:    tataḥ prasannā yoginyo jīvayitvā janeśvaram:
35:    yathābhilaṣitaṃ rājan varaṃ vṛṇv ity avādiṣuḥ.
36:    tataḥ sa varayām āsa mastakasthāpitāñjaliḥ:
37:    gṛhāḥ sapta pratidinaṃ svarṇapūrṇā bhavantv iti.
38:    evaṃ tvayā kṛte nityam, evam eva bhaviṣyati.
39:    iti dattvā varaṃ rājñe yoginyo 'dṛśyatāṃ yayuḥ.
40:    rājā 'pi pratyahaṃ samyak svadehavyayasādhitaiḥ
41:    dhanair arthijanābhīṣṭaṃ vyadhāt saptagṛhasthitaiḥ.
42:    kadācid etadvṛttāntaṃ vikramādityabhūpatiḥ

45:    asya dainaṃdinaṃ duḥkhaṃ mā bhud iti vicārayan,
46:    homaśālāṃ samāsādya manasā yoginīḥ smaran,

Metrical Recension of 18
MR:
Version: MR

Paragraph: 1

1:    punaḥ kadācit saṃprāptam āsanārohanecchayā
2:    bhojarājam samālokya babhāṣe sālabhañjikā:
3:    sāhasaṃ dhairyam audāryaṃ syāt sadā tādṛśaṃ nṛpa,
4:    sa evā 'rhaty avasthātuṃ pauruhūte mahāsane.
5:    bhojarājas tato 'pṛcchat: tat kīdṛg iti tāṃ punaḥ;
6:    ākarṇaye 'ti vyācaṣṭa kathāṃ karṇarasāyanīm.
7:    asti vismāritāśeṣamahīpālamahāyaśāḥ,
8:    śaśāsa vikramādityaḥ sa ratnākaramekhalām;
9:    nā 'dharmaśīlā nā 'śūrā nā 'prajā nā 'bahuśrutāḥ,
10:    na 'narthā nā 'nayajñāś ca yena saṃrakṣitāḥ prajāḥ.
11:    adharmasya ca saṃcāraṃ nayasya ca viparyayam
12:    vihāya, pālito rājñā kalikālaḥ kṛtīkṛtaḥ.
13:    taṃ kadācin mahīpālaṃ kaścit siddho mahāmatiḥ
14:    dṛṣṭvā 'śiṣaḥ prayuñjānas tadādeśād upāviśat.
15:    bruhi dṛṣṭaṃ kiṃ āścaryam iti prītyā pracoditaḥ,
16:    avādīd avanībhartre dṛṣṭam āścaryam ātmanā.
17:    udayādreḥ samīpe 'sti nagaraṃ kanakaprabham;
18:    tatra devālayo devadevasyā 'sti vivasvataḥ.
19:    tatpuraḥ pravahaty ekā sarit sūryaprabhābhidhā.
20:    kṛtārthitārthisārthaṃ tat tīrthaṃ pāpavināśanam,
21:    candrakāntaśilākrāntaracanācitritakramam,
22:    caturdikkalpitavanaprāsādaprakarāvṛtam.
23:    tasminn agādhasalile tīrthamadhye sthirāsanaḥ
24:    śatakumbhamayastambho vidyate devanirmitaḥ.
25:    udety anudinaṃ deva dinabhartur anū 'dayam,
26:    sa modamānas tadbimbaṃ madhyāhne saṃspṛśaty asau.
27:    punar apy anuvṛttyai 'va pratyag āvartanāt saha
28:    tajjale majjati stambhaḥ pratyag gacchaty ahaskare.
29:    etad ālokitaṃ deva divyatīrthe mahādbhutam;
30:    kiṃ-kiṃ nā 'sti kṣitau dhātur acintyā sṛṣṭicāturī.
31:    iti siddhavacaḥ śrutvā sasamṛddhaikasāhasaḥ
32:    samutkaṇṭhaḥ sa taṃ draṣṭuṃ rājakanṭhīravo yayau.
33:    kanakaṭṭālakopetaṃ kanatkanakagopuram
34:    dadarśa 'sau puraṃ rājā kanakaprabhasaṃjñikam
35:    tataḥ sūryaprabhākhyā yā prakhyātā pāpanāśinī,
36:    sasnau kallolamālinyām anubhāvitamānasaḥ.
37:    ādityeśvaram abhyarcya puṣpaiḥ paśupatiṃ śuciḥ,
38:    upoṣya tasthau niyato rātrau taddevatālaye,
39:    uṣasy utthāya sumanas tirthe pāpavināśane
40:    kṛtānuṣṭhānaniyamaḥ pūjayitvā divākaram;
41:    etasminn eva samaye tanmadhyād udabhāt kṣaṇāt
42:    sa stambho 'pi mahībhartuḥ purastād eva kāñcanaḥ.
43:    tato laghutayo 'tpatya 'tanmadhye praviveśa saḥ,
44:    vegād agād api stambho nabhomadhyagataṃ ravim.
45:    pluṣṭaḥ pataṅgakiranair yajuṣā 'staut sa taṃ nṛpaḥ.
46:    samīpe saṃstuvantam taṃ dadarśa stambhamadhyagam,

47:    vinataṃ mahasām īśo mastakasthāpitāñjalim,
48:    anvagrahīt tam āpluṣṭam āpannārtiharo raviḥ,
49:    uvāca priyayā vācā: maheśvaravaśād bhavān
50:    bhadra jīvasi, kiṃ jīvet karadagdho jano 'nyathā?
51:    gṛhāṇa - tava tuṣṭo 'smi - kuṇḍaladvitayaṃ mama,
52:    yatpadmarāgaprabhayā mayy abhūd aruṇaprabhā,
53:    dine-dine bhāravarṣe suvarṇānāṃ suvarcasām.
54:    iti dattvā yayau devas tasmai tat kuṇḍaladvayam.
55:    tajjale 'majjata stambhād astaṃgacchaty ahaskare,
56:    tasya mūlaṃ ca jijñāsur adhastād avaruhya saḥ,
57:    rasātale tu tanmūle devadevasya bhāsvataḥ
58:    prabhādevīṃ priyāṃ lokamātaraṃ saṃdadarśa saḥ.
59:    sūryapatnīṃ tu tāṃ natvā tasthau sa vinayānvitaḥ;
60:    tataḥ prītyā prabhādevī yatheṣṭābharaṇapradām
61:    maṇiṃ divyāṃ dadau tasmai; so 'pi natvā muhur-muhuḥ,
62:    devyāḥ sakāśān niṣkramya bahis, tasyās tu saṃnidhau
63:    suvarṇavedikāmadhye dīptastambhāyutaṃ niśi
64:    tam eva kāñcanastambhaṃ dṛṣṭvā, jñātvā ca tadguṇam,
65:    tasyo 'pari samāruhya punaḥ pratyuṣasi prabhuḥ,
66:    sūryodaye yathāpūrvaṃ tasmin sūryaprabhotthite,
67:    avaplutya nṛpaḥ stambhāt *prāpa *puṣkariṇītaṭam.
68:    tatrai 'va dvādaśādityamaṇḍape maṇḍaleśvaraḥ,
69:    vidhivat pāraṇāṃ kṛtvā, gacchan pathi mahāmanāḥ,
70:    sapatnīkaṃ kṛśaṃ dīnaṃ yācamānaṃ mahīsuram
71:    dīnānukampano rājā jagade janayan mudam:
72:    ime vaikartane vipra kuṇḍale ratnanirmite
73:    dine-dine suvarṇānāṃ jānīthā bhāravarṣinī;
74:    maṇiś cā 'yaṃ mahātejāḥ prabhādevyāḥ prasādataḥ
75:    abhīṣṭābharaṇaṃ datte, gṛhāṇai 'kaṃ tvam etayoḥ,
76:    ekam asyai 'va bhāryāyai prayacche 'ti prasannadhīḥ.
77:    jñāpayitve 'ti sāmarthyaṃ saṃpradāya dvijātaye
78:    te ratnakuṇḍale rājā jagāmo 'jjaylnīm purīm.
79:    evaṃ sāhasam audāryaṃ dhairyaṃ ca bhavato yadi,
80:    āroha bhojabhūpāla tadīyam idam āsanam.

81:    ity astādaśī kathā

20. Story of the Twentieth Statuette
Vikrama visits a forest ascetic

Paragraph: 1

1:    punar api rājā yāvat siṅhāsana upaviśati, tāvad anyā puttalikā
2:     'bravīt: bho rājan, yasya vikramasyau 'dāryādiguṇā bhavanti, so
3:     'smin siṅhāsana upaviśatu. rājā 'vadat: bhoḥ puttalike, kathaya
4:    tasyau 'dāryavṛttāntam. sā 'bravīt: bho rājan, śrūyatām.
5:    vikramo rājā ṣaṇmāsaṃ rājyaṃ karoti, ṣaṇmāsaṃ deśāntaraṃ
6:    gacchati. ekadā deśāntaragato nānādeśān paribhramya padmālayaṃ
7:    nāma nagaram agamat. tannagarād bahir udyānavane 'tivimalo/dakaṃ
8:    sarovaraṃ dṛṣṭvā tatro 'dakapānaṃ vidhāyo 'paviṣṭaḥ. tato
9:     'nye kecana vaideśikāḥ kecana svadeśikā āgatya jalapānaṃ vidhāyo
10:     'paviṣṭāḥ, parasparaṃ goṣṭhīṃ kurvanti: aho asmābhir anekadeśā
11:    dṛṣṭāḥ, bahūni tīrthāni dṛṣṭāni, atidurgamāḥ kairapy anadhigamyāḥ
12:    parvatā ārūḍhāḥ, param ekatrā 'pi mahāpuruṣadarśanaṃ nā 'bhūt.
13:    anyena bhaṇitam: kathaṃ mahāpuruṣadarśanaṃ bhaviṣyati? yatra
14:    mahāsiddho 'sti, tatra gantum aśakyam; mārgo durgamaḥ; madhye
15:     'nekavighnāḥ; dehasyā 'pi nāśo bhavati. yeno 'dyamena prathamam
16:    ātmanāśaṃ prāpnoti, tasya phalaṃ ko 'nubhaviṣyati? ataḥ kāraṇāt
17:    prathamam ātmai 'va rakṣaṇīyo buddhimatā. uktaṃ ca: śarīram
18:    ādyaṃ khalu dharmasādhanam iti. tathā ca:

10:    tatrai 'kasmin mṛttikā, dvitīye 'ṅgārakāḥ, tṛtīye 'sthīni, caturthe tuṣāḥ. etatpara/mārtham
11:    ajānānais tair bahavo lokāḥ pṛṣṭāḥ, paraṃ ko 'pi na jānāti. anyadā vikrama/sabhāyāṃ
12:    taiḥ pṛṣṭam; tatrā 'pi na nirṇayo jātaḥ. tatas te pratiṣṭhānapure gatāḥ,
13:    tatrā 'pi na kenāpi nirṇayaḥ kṛtaḥ. atrāntare pratiṣṭhānapure vipradvayam asti.
14:    tadbhaginī vidhavā rūpasvinī kenāpi nāgakumāreṇa bhuktā gurviṇī jātā. tāṃ tathā/bhūtāṃ
15:    dṛṣṭvā parasparaṃ śaṅkitau dvāv api deśāntaraṃ gatau. sā ca nāgakumāra/sāṃnidhyāt
16:    sthitā prasūtā, putro jātaḥ, tasyā 'bhidhānaṃ śālivāhanaḥ. sa ca mātrā
17:    yutaḥ kumbhakāragṛhe tiṣṭhati. sa ca tad vivādasvarūpaṃ śrutvā sabhāyām āgatya
18:    prāha, yathā: bhoḥ sabhyāḥ, etadvādanirṇayam ahaṃ kariṣye. tadā sāścaryaṃ
19:    sarvair vilokyamānaḥ prāha: yasya pitrā mṛttikā dattā, tasya sarvā bhūmiḥ; yasya
20:    tuṣā dattāḥ, tasya sakalaṃ dhānyam; yasyā 'sthīni, tasya sarvaṃ dvipadacatuḥpa/dādikam;
21:    yasyā 'ṅgārakā dattāḥ, tasya suvarṇādayaḥ saptā 'pi dhātavaḥ. etad
22:    ākarṇya sarve pramuditāḥ, bhagno vivādaḥ; te catvāro 'pi svagṛhaṃ gatāḥ. etan/nirṇayasvarūpam
23:    ākarṇya śrīvikrameṇa tasya śiśor āhvānaṃ pratiṣṭhānapure preṣi/tam;
24:    paraṃ sa nā 'yāti, kathayati ca: kasmād ahaṃ tasya pārśve yāsyāmi? yadi
25:    kāryaṃ bhaviṣyati, tarhi sa evā 'tra sameṣyati. etad ākarṇya saparikaro vikra/manṛpaḥ
26:    pratiṣṭhānaṃ prati calitaḥ. tadā 'pi lokaiḥ preryamāṇo 'pi sa nā 'yāti.
27:    tataḥ puraṃ ruddhaṃ vikrameṇa. tadā tasya śiśoḥ krīḍayā kṛtā mṛnmayā gajatura/gapadātayo
28:    nāgakumāraprabhāvāt sajīvāḥ saṃgrāmāyo 'tthitāḥ. paraṃ tair vikramo
29:    na bhagnaḥ. tataḥ svaputrapakṣapātena nāgakumāreṇa rātrau vikramasainyaṃ
30:    daṣṭaṃ mūrchitaṃ bhūmau patitam. tat tathā dṛṣṭvā vikrameṇa vāsukirājamantrārā/dhanaṃ
31:    kṛtam. tena ca tuṣtena rājño 'mṛtaṃ dattam. tad gṛhītvā yāvad vikramaḥ
32:    sainye samāyāti, tāvat puruṣadvayenā 'gatya prārthitaḥ prāha: kiṃ yacchāmi?
33:    tābhyām uktam. amṛtaṃ dehī 'ti. tato rājñā pṛṣṭam: kau yuvām? tābhyām uktam:
34:    āvām śāīivāhanena preṣitau. tato rājñā cintitam: yady apy etau vairiṇā preṣitau,
35:    tathā 'pi yan mayā pratipannaṃ tad deyam eve 'ti dattam amṛtam. tatas tatsattvena
36:    tuṣṭaḥ punar api vāsukināgas tat sainyaṃ kṣaṇād utthāpitavān, śrivikramanṛpaṃ ca
37:    tuṣṭāva. uktam ca:

Emboxt story: The prince who insulted a brahman
19:    himavato dakṣinapārśve vindhyavatī nāma nagarī. tatra, suvicāro
20:    nāma rājā prativasati. tasya putro jayasenaḥ. sa ekadā 'kheṭanār
21:    thaṃ vanaṃ gataḥ. vane karinam ekaṃ dṛṣṭvā tadanugato mahā/vanaṃ
22:    praviṣṭaḥ. yathā kathaṃcin nagaramārgaṃ gata āsīt, tata
23:    ekākī yāvad āgacchati, tāvad vanamadhya ekā nadī dṛṣṭā. tatra
24:    nadītaṭe kaśid brāhmaṇo 'nuṣṭhānaṃ karoti. rājaputras tatsamīpaṃ
25:    gatvā 'vadat: bho brāhmaṇa, yāvad ahaṃ jalapānaṃ vidhāsyāmi,
26:    tāvad amum aśvaṃ gṛhāṇa. brāhmaṇeno 'ktam: ahaṃ kiṃ tava
27:    preṣyo 'śvaṃ dhārayiṣāmi? tatas tena kaśayā tāḍitaḥ. brāhmaṇo
28:    rudan rājasamīpaṃ āgatya nivedayām āsa. rājā 'pi krodhāruṇa/locanaḥ
29:    san putraṃ svadeśān nirghāṭayām āsa. tasminn avasare
30:    mantriṇā bhaṇitam: he deva, rājyabhārodvahanayogyaḥ kumāraḥ

Tales peculiar to single recensions of the Vikrama-carita
These are given on the following pages, 229-240. they are:
Metrical Recension (mr 32): Bhaṭṭi becomes Vikrama 's minister 229
Jainistic Recension (jr v): Vikrama wins the kingdom from Agnivetāla 233

Jainistic Recension (jr ix): Brilliancy of Vikrama 's court 236
Jainistic Recension (jr 29): Vikrama and the sign-reader 238
Jainistic Recension (jr 31): The haunted house   239
Jainistic Recension (jr 32): The poverty-statue 240
As to their position in the manuscripts of mr and jr, see my introduction, volume 26, part ii, and also the table on page xii of this volume.

Section v (of mss.) of the Jainistic Recension
Vikrama wins the kingdom from Agnivetāla
JR:

Paragraph: 1

1:    tatas tad avantīrājyaṃ śūnyam ekenā 'gnivetālanāmnā devenā 'dhiṣṭhitam; tatra
2:    yaṃ-yaṃ navīnaṃ rājānaṃ kurvanti mantrinas taṃ-taṃ sa rātrau mārayati; kenāpy
3:    upāyena na śāmyati. tataḥ kiṃkartavyatāmūḍho 'bhūd rājavargaḥ. atrāntare
4:    deśāntarāt sāmānyavṛttyā vikramādityenā 'nupalakṣyamāṇenā 'gatya mantriṇaḥ
5:    proktāḥ: kim idaṃ rājyaṃ śūnyam? iti. tais tasyā 'gre vetālasvarūpaṃ proktam.
6:    teno 'ktam: tarhi mām adya rājānaṃ kuruta. taiś ca sattvādhiko 'yam iti sa rājā
7:    kṛtaḥ. tena ca sakalaṃ dinaṃ rājyalīlām anubhūya saṃdhyāsamaye nijaśayyāsamīpe
8:    sarvato 'nekopahārayukto baliḥ kāritaḥ, svayaṃ ca śayyāyāṃ jāgarūkaḥ sthitaḥ.
9:    tāvad āyātaḥ kālavikarālarūpo vetālaḥ samantād baliṃ dṛṣṭvo 'rdhvaṃ sthitaḥ,
10:    khaḍgam ādāya vadhāyā 'gacchan vikrameṇa proktaḥ, yathā: bhoḥ, pūrvaṃ baliṃ
11:    gṛhāṇa, paścād apy ahaṃ tvadāyatto 'smi. tataḥ sa baliṃ gṛhītvā saṃtuṣṭaḥ prāha:
12:    bhoḥ sāttvika, dattaṃ mayā tava rājyam, paraṃ pratyahaṃ tvayā mahyaṃ baliḥ
13:    kārya iti kathayitvā gato vetālaḥ. tataḥ prabhāte mantriṇo rājānaṃ jīvitaṃ dṛṣṭvā
14:    hṛṣṭāḥ prāhuḥ: aho sattvādhikaśiromaṇir ayam.
15:    evaṃ pratyahaṃ vetālaḥ samāyāti baliṃ gṛhnāti. anyadā rājñā pṛṣṭam: bho
16:    vetāla, tava kiyatī śaktiḥ, kiyaj jñānam? teno 'ce: ahaṃ yac cintayāmi tat karomi,
17:    sarvaṃ jānāmī 'ti. tato rājñā proktam: mamā 'yuḥ kiyatpramāṇam? sa ca prāha:
18:    tava śatavarṣam āyuḥ. nṛpeṇo 'ktam: mamā 'yuṣi śūnyaṃ patitam, tarhi tvayā
19:    varṣam ekaṃ samadhikaṃ nyūnaṃ vā karaṇīyam. tataḥ sa prāha: tavā 'yuḥ kenāpi
20:    samadhikaṃ nyūnaṃ vā na bhavati. tato baliṃ lātvā gato vetālaḥ. punar dvitīye
21:    dine balim akṛtvā sthitaṃ rājānam avekṣya cukopa: re tvayā 'dya kasmān na bali/vidhānaṃ
22:    kṛtam? rājño 'ce: yadi mamā 'yuḥ kenāpy adhikaṃ nyūnaṃ vā na
23:    syāt, tarhi kimartham ahaṃ pratyahaṃ baliṃ karomi? uttiṣṭha mayā saha ranāye
24:     'ti khaḍgam ādāyā 'gre sthito rājā. tatas tasya sattvena tuṣṭo vetālaḥ prāha: bho
25:    rājan, sattvādhika, yācasva kimapi varam, yato 'moghaṃ devadarśanam. tato
26:    rājño 'ktam: yadi tuṣṭo 'si, tarhi yadā 'haṃ tvāṃ smarāmi, tadā tvayā 'gantavyaṃ
27:    matkāryaṃ ca vidheyam. pratipannaṃ tad devena, gataḥ svaṃ sthānam. tataḥ
28:    prabhāte mantribhir vikramādityasya mahatā mahena rājyābhisekaś cakre.

25:    rājñā proktam. bhoḥ śreṣṭhin, yadi tvaṃ tatra bibheṣi tarhi yad dravyaṃ tatra
26:    saudhe tava lagnam, tat tvaṃ gṛhāṇe 'ti śrutvā pramuditaḥ śreṣṭhī kim anena prāṇa/saṃdehakāriṇā
27:    saudhene 'ti rājñā dattaṃ yathāpramaṇaṃ mūlyadravyam ādāya
28:    svagṛhaṃ gataḥ.
29:    tataḥ saṃdhyāsamaye kṛtadānapuṇyaḥ śrīvikramaḥ samagrarājavarganiṣidh/yamānaḥ
30:    svasattvabalena tatra saudhe gataḥ. palyaṅke yāvac chete tāvad devaḥ
31:    prāha: bhoḥ patāmi. tato rājñā 'bhayena proktam: śighraṃ pata, mā vilambaṃ
32:    kuru. tatas tadbhāgyena patitaḥ suvarṇamayaḥ puruṣaḥ, pratyakṣībhūya tadadhiṣ/ṭhāyako
33:    devaḥ puṣpavṛstiṃ kṛtvā prabhāvaṃ prakāśya rājānaṃ praśasya svasthānaṃ
34:    gataḥ. rājā tu prabhāte mahatā mahena suvarṇamayaṃ puruṣam ādāya svasaudham
35:    agāt.
36:    ato rājann īdrśam audāryaṃ yadi tvayi syāt, tadā 'smin si√hāsane tvam upaviśa.

37:    iti si√hāsanadvātri√śakāyām ekatri√śatkathā

Tales peculiar to single Recensions of the Vikrama-carita
Story 32 (of mss.) of the Jainistic Recension - The poverty-statue
JR:

Paragraph: 1

1:    punar aparamuhūrte bhojarājaḥ sakalām abhiṣekasāmagrīṃ kṛṭvā yāvat si√hāsa/nam
2:    adhirohati, tāvad dvātri√śattamā putrikā 'vadat: rājan, asmin si√hāsane sa
3:    upaviśati, yasya vikramādityasadṛśam audāryaṃ bhavati. kīdṛśaṃ tad audāryam
4:    iti rājñā pṛṣṭa putrikā prāha: rājan,
5:    avantīpuryāṃ śrīvikramanṛpaḥ sāmrājyaṃ karoti. anyada 'vantīpratyāsannagrā/māt
6:    ko 'pi vaṇikputro 'vantyāṃ vāṇijyāya samāyātaḥ. tatratyaṃ svarūpaṃ dṛṣṭvā
7:    vismitaḥ svagrāmaṃ gatvā nijapitur akathayat, yathā: tāta, avantyāṃ yat kiṃcit
8:    kriyānakam āyāti, tat sarvaṃ lokaḥ śīghraṃ gṛhnāti; yat tiṣṭhati, tat sarvaṃ saṃ/dhyāyām
9:    rājā gṛhnāti, yato 'trāyātavastunaḥ ko 'pi na krete 'ti mā nagarasya kalaṅko
10:    bhūt. etad ākarṇya tatpitā dhūrto lohamayaṃ putrakam ekaṃ kārayitvā tasya ca
11:    dāridram iti nāma prakalpyā 'vantyāṃ gataḥ sthito rājamārge kenāpi pṛṣṭo vakti:
12:    dāridraṃ vikretum ānītam asti. kiṃ mūlyam iti pṛṣṭo dīnārasahasraṃ vakti. etad
13:    ākarṇya ko 'pi taṃ dāridraputrakaṃ na gṛhṇāti. tataḥ saṃdhyāyāṃ rājādeśena
14:    gṛhīto rājapuruṣaiḥ; dattaṃ tasya mūlyam. tataḥ sa dāridraputrakaḥ kṣiptaḥ kośe.
15:    tato rātrau dāridram āyātaṃ dṛṣṭvā saptāṅgarājyalakṣmīḥ saptamūrtimayī raṇan/maṇimekhalāmālabhāriṇī
16:    rājñaḥ pratyakṣā 'bhūt. tato rājā sasaṃbhramaṃ samut/thāya
17:    praṇāmāñjalipūrvaṃ bhagavatīṃ laksmīṃ tuṣṭāva, yathā:

Strophe: 1

a:    huntīi hunti aṇahuntayā vi, jantīi janti huntā vi,

Paragraph: 2

1:    iti stutvā pratyakṣāgamanakāranaṃ papraccha. tato lakṣmīḥ prāha: rājan, ahaṃ
2:    yāsyāmi; tava kośe dāridram āyātam. tato rājñā proktam: devi, yat sāṃsārikaṃ
3:    sukhaṃ tat sarvaṃ tvadanugrahādhīnam iti tvaṃ mā yāhi. tato lakṣmīḥ prāha:
4:    yatra dāridraṃ tatra 'haṃ na kathamapi tiṣṭhāmī 'ti śrutvā rājño 'ktam: yan mayā
5:    dāridraputrakaḥ svīkṛtaḥ, sa svīkṛta eva, tan nā 'nyathā. tvaṃ yadi yāsyasi, tarhi
6:    yāhi 'ti śrutvā gatā lakṣmīḥ. tataḥ kṣaṇantare samāyāto vivekaḥ prāha. bho rajan,
7:    yatra dāridraṃ tatra nā 'smākaṃ sthitir iti gatā lakṣmīḥ, aham api yāsyāmi. tato
8:    rājñā sthāpito 'py atiṣṭhan rājānam anujñāpya gato vivekaḥ. tataḥ punaḥ kṣaṇāntare
9:    samāyātaṃ sattvaṃ rājānam abhāṣata. rājan, yatra dāridraṃ tatra vayaṃ na
10:    tiṣṭhāmaḥ; ata eva purā gatau lakṣmīvivekau, tvaṃ ciraparicitam anujñāpanāya
11:    samāyāto 'smi, param aham api yāsyāmi. etad ākarṇya rājā sasaṃbhrāntaś cinti/tavān:
12:    aho yadi puruṣasya sattvaṃ gatam, tarhi kim sthitam? yataḥ:

Strophe: 4

a:    prayātu lakṣmīś capalasvabhāvā,
b:    guṇā vivekapramukhāḥ prayāntu,
c:    prāṇāś ca gacchantu kṛtaprayāṇā;
d:    mā yātu sattvaṃ tu nṛṇāṃ kadācit. 4

Verse:

Paragraph: 3

1:    tato rājñā proktam. bhoḥ sattva, sarvam apy aparaṃ yātu, paraṃ tvaṃ mā yāhi.
2:    tataḥ sattvaṃ prāha: rājan, yatra dāridraṃ tatra nā 'haṃ kathamapi tiṣṭhāmī 'ti.
3:    rājño 'ktam: tarhi gṛhāne 'daṃ mamo 'ttamāṅgam; tvam vinā prāṇaiḥ kiṃ prayo/janam
4:    iti khaḍgam ādāya yāvac chiraśchedaṃ karoti, tāvat sattvena rājā kare dhṛtaḥ.
5:    tataḥ sthitaṃ sattvam; tataḥ samāyātau tatsahacāriṇau lakṣmīvivekau.
6:    ato rājann īdṛśaṃ sattvaṃ yadi tvayi syat, tadā 'smin si√hāsane tvam upaviśa.
